Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The radioactivity variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 974 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 53 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to radioactivity theory, we decompose the problem into 974 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 53. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
